# Sydney - Tech recruiter shortlist

Reviewed 7 November 2026. Added 91 recruiters to [Sydney - Tech](https://www.linkedin.com/talent/hire/2159955370/manage/all). The existing project uses this name rather than Tech - Sydney. Verified 91 active, uncontacted candidates; preserved 11 existing archived candidates. No messages sent.

Criteria: Sydney/Greater Sydney and Sydney suburbs; currently employed by a recruitment agency in technology recruitment; Recruitment Consultant, Senior Consultant, Principal Consultant or Managing Consultant, including senior/principal consultant variants. At least 14 months continuous tenure at the current company, including promotions. Cutoff: 7 September 2025; ambiguous September 2025 starts were held back. Current Sirius, The Onset, Just Digital People and Preacta employees excluded. Standing founder, owner, co-founder, NED and C-suite exclusions applied.

Coverage: reviewed cards across seven focused search pages, 18 current/past agency expansion pages, and three final agency expansion pages. Search totals are overlapping discovery results, not counts of full profiles reviewed. Selected profiles were opened to check current titles, companies, dates, technology remit and project status. Agency history expansion included 89 initial agency names/aliases and 27 additional names/aliases. Generic, contradictory or unverified leads were left unsaved. No exhaustive-market claim.

Verification: pipeline showed 91 uncontacted and 11 archived. Reconciled 90 names from the saved list and verified the remaining candidate directly through current-project membership. Existing stages in other projects were left unchanged. Profiles are self-reported LinkedIn information; some older or thin profiles may require freshness checks before outreach. Some qualifying desks span technology and other disciplines, including transformation, cyber risk, digital/design and SaaS commercial hiring; the evidence notes below identify broader remits where recorded.
